\mathbb{E}\left[(L_{t}^{d}-L_{s}^{d})^{4}\right]\leq C(t-s)^{2}.

(313)

The case
|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}|=|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}s\rfloor,1}|<\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2
follows using the same strategy and exploiting the boundedness of the moments of normal distributions and the boundedness of
X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}
itself.

For all
0\leq s\leq t
such that
\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil\leq\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor
, we can distinguish three cases.

Case 1

If
|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}|\geq\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2
and
|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}s\rfloor,1}|\geq\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2
, then

\displaystyle L_{t}^{d}-L_{s}^{d}

\displaystyle=X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}-X^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil,1}

(314)

\displaystyle+(d^{2\alpha}t-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor)\left[\sigma_{d}Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil,1}-\frac{\sigma^{2}_{d}}{2}\sgn(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1})\right]\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil}}

(315)

\displaystyle+(\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil-d^{2\alpha}s)\left[\sigma_{d}Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil,1}-\frac{\sigma^{2}_{d}}{2}\sgn(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}s\rfloor,1})\right]\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil}}.

(316)

Using Hölder’s inequality (
307
) collecting all constants in
C
, and the fact that
0\leq d^{2\alpha}t-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor\leq 1
(and similarly for
s
) we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[(L_{t}^{d}-L_{s}^{d})^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\left[\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}-X^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil,1}\right)^{4}\right]

(317)

\displaystyle+C\frac{(d^{2\alpha}t-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor)^{2}}{d^{4\alpha}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\ell Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil,1}\right)^{4}+\frac{\ell^{8}}{2^{4}d^{4\alpha}}\right]

(318)

\displaystyle+C\frac{(\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil-d^{2\alpha}s)^{2}}{d^{4\alpha}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\ell Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil,1}\right)^{4}+\frac{\ell^{8}}{2^{4}d^{4\alpha}}\right].

(319)

Recalling that the moments of
Z^{d}
are bounded and that
d^{2\alpha}s\leq\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil\leq\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor\leq d^{2\alpha}t
, it follows

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[(L_{t}^{d}-L_{s}^{d})^{4}\right]\leq C\left((t-s)^{2}+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}-X^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil,1}\right)^{4}\right]\right).

(320)

Case 2

If
|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}|\geq\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2
and
|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}s\rfloor,1}|<\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2
or
|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}|<\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2
and
|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}s\rfloor,1}|\geq\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2
.
We only describe the argument for the first case, the second case follows from analogous steps.
Take

\displaystyle L_{t}^{d}-L_{s}^{d}

\displaystyle=X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}+(d^{2\alpha}t-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor)\left[\sigma_{d}Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil,1}-\frac{\sigma^{2}_{d}}{2}\sgn(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1})\right]\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil}}

(321)

\displaystyle-X^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil,1}-(\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil-d^{2\alpha}s)\left(\sigma_{d}Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil,1}-\frac{1}{\sigma_{d}^{2(m-1)}r}X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}s\rfloor,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil}}.

(322)

Proceeding as above, we find that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[(L_{t}^{d}-L_{s}^{d})^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\left((t-s)^{2}+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}-X^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil,1}\right)^{4}\right]\right.

(323)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.+(\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil-d^{2\alpha}s)^{4}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sigma_{d}^{2(m-1)}r}X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}s\rfloor}\right)^{4}\right]\right),

(324)

and recalling that
|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}s\rfloor,1}|<\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2
we have that
|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}s\rfloor,1}|/(r\sigma_{d}^{2(m-1)})<\sigma_{d}^{2}/2
. Using this and the same arguments as above, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[(L_{t}^{d}-L_{s}^{d})^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\left((t-s)^{2}+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}-X^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil,1}\right)^{4}\right]\right).

(325)

Case 3

If
|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}|<\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2
and
|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}s\rfloor,1}|<\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2
, then

\displaystyle L_{t}^{d}-L_{s}^{d}

\displaystyle=X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}+(d^{2\alpha}t-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor)\left(\sigma_{d}Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil,1}-\frac{1}{\sigma_{d}^{2(m-1)}r}X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil}}

(326)

\displaystyle-X^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil,1}+(\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil-d^{2\alpha}s)\left(\sigma_{d}Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil,1}-\frac{1}{\sigma_{d}^{2(m-1)}r}X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}s\rfloor,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil}}.

(327)

Using the boundedness of moments of Gaussian distributions and of
X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1},X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}s\rfloor,1}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[(L_{t}^{d}-L_{s}^{d})^{4}\right]\leq C\left((t-s)^{2}+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}-X^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil,1}\right)^{4}\right]\right).

(328)

Putting (
320
), (
325
) and (
328
) together and using Lemma
1
below we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(L_{t}^{d}-L_{s}^{d}\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left(t-s\right)^{2}+\sum_{p=2}^{4}\frac{\left(\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor-\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil\right)^{p}}{d^{2\alpha p}}\right)

(329)

\displaystyle\leq C(t-s)^{2}+C\sum_{p=2}^{4}\frac{d^{2\alpha p}\left(t-s\right)^{p}}{d^{2\alpha p}}\leq C\left(2+t+t^{2}\right)(t-s)^{2},

(330)

which concludes the proof.
∎

We are now ready to state and prove Lemma
1
:

Lemma 1
.

There exists
C>0
such that for any
k_{1},k_{2}\in\mathbb{N}
with
0\leq k_{1}<k_{2}
,

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(X^{d}_{k_{2},1}-X^{d}_{k_{1},1}\right)^{4}\right]\leq C\sum_{p=2}^{4}\frac{(k_{2}-k_{1})^{p}}{d^{2\alpha p}},

(331)

where
\alpha=1/3
.

Proof.

Recalling the definition of the proposal in (
303
)
and the definition of
b_{d}
in (
286
) we can write

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(X_{k_{2},1}^{d}-X^{d}_{k_{1},1}\right)^{4}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{k=k_{1}+1}^{k_{2}}\sigma_{d}b_{d}\left(X_{k-1,1}^{d},Z^{d}_{k,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{k}^{d}}\right)^{4}\right].

(332)

Then, we expand all acceptance or rejection terms between
k_{1}
and
k_{2}
and use Hölder’s inequality to obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(X_{k_{2},1}^{d}-X^{d}_{k_{1},1}\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\left\{\sigma_{d}^{4}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{k=k_{1}+1}^{k_{2}}b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{k-1,1},Z^{d}_{k,1}\right)\right)^{4}\right]\right.

(333)

\displaystyle\left.+\sigma_{d}^{4}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{k=k_{1}+1}^{k_{2}}b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{k-1,1},Z^{d}_{k,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{(\mathsf{A}_{k}^{d})^{c}}\right)^{4}\right]\right\}.

(334)

Using again Hölder’s inequality, for the first term we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{k=k_{1}+1}^{k_{2}}b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{k-1,1},Z^{d}_{k,1}\right)\right)^{4}\right]\leq C\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{k=k_{1}+1}^{k_{2}}Z^{d}_{k,1}\right)^{4}\right]\right.

(335)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\sigma_{d}^{4}}{2^{4}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{k=k_{1}+1}^{k_{2}}\sgn\left(X^{d}_{k-1,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}\left\{|X_{k-1,1}^{d}|\geq\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2\right\}\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{k=k_{1}+1}^{k_{2}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{d}^{2v-1}r}X^{d}_{k-1,1}\mathbbm{1}\left\{|X_{k-1,1}^{d}|<\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2\right\}\right)^{4}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C\left[3(k_{2}-k_{1})^{2}+\frac{2\sigma_{d}^{4}}{2^{4}}(k_{2}-k_{1})^{4}\right],

(336)

where the last line follows using the moments of
Z^{d}_{k,1}
and the boundedness of
X_{k-1,1}^{d}
in the set
\{|X_{k-1,1}^{d}|<\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2\}
.

Using a Binomial expansion of the rejection term, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{k=k_{1}+1}^{k_{2}}b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{k-1,1},Z^{d}_{k,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\mathsf{A}^{d}_{k}\right)^{c}}\right)^{4}\right]=\sum\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{i=1}^{4}b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{m_{i}-1,1},Z^{d}_{m_{i},1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\mathsf{A}^{d}_{m_{i}}\right)^{c}}\right],

(337)

where the sum is over the quadruplets
(m_{i})_{1\leq i\leq 4}
with
m_{i}\in\left\{k_{1}+1,\dots,k_{2}\right\}
.

We separate the terms in the sum according to their cardinality: we denote by
|B|
the cardinality of set
B
(i.e. the number of distinct elements), and, for
j\in\{1,\dots,4\}
,
we define

\mathcal{I}_{j}=\left\{(m_{1},\dots,m_{4})\in\left\{k_{1}+1,\dots,k_{2}\right\}^{4}:|\left\{m_{1},\dots,m_{4}\right\}|=j\right\}.

(338)

For any
(m_{1},\dots,m_{4})\in\left\{k_{1}+1,\dots,k_{2}\right\}^{4}
,
\widetilde{X}^{d}_{0}=X^{d}_{0}
and for any
i\in\{1,\dots,d\}
,

\displaystyle\widetilde{X}^{d}_{k+1,i}=\widetilde{X}^{d}_{k,i}+\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{m_{1}-1,\dots,m_{4}-1\right\}^{c}}(k)\mathbbm{1}_{\widetilde{\mathsf{A}}_{k+1}^{d}}\sigma_{d}b_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}_{k,i}^{d},Z^{d}_{k+1,i}\right),

(339)

where

\widetilde{\mathsf{A}}_{k+1}^{d}=\left\{U_{k+1}\leq\exp\left[\sum_{i=1}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}^{d}_{k,i},Z_{k+1,i}^{d}\right)\right]\right\},

(340)

and
\phi_{d}
in (
287
).
Denote by
\mathcal{F}
the
\sigma
-algebra generated by the process

(\widetilde{X}_{k}^{d})_{k\geq 0}
and observe that on the event

\underset{j=1}{\overset{4}{\bigcap}}\left(\mathsf{A}_{m_{j}}^{d}\right)^{c}
,
X_{k}^{d}
is
equal to
\widetilde{X}_{k}^{d}
.

We consider now the terms in the sum (
337
).

1.

If
(m_{1},\dots,m_{4})\in\mathcal{I}_{4}
, then the
m_{i}
s are all distinct and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{4}b_{d}\left(X_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d},Z_{m_{j},1}^{d}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\mathsf{A}_{m_{j}}^{d}\right)^{c}}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{4}b_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d},Z^{d}_{m_{j},1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\widetilde{\mathsf{A}}_{m_{j}}^{d}\right)^{c}}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right].

(341)

However,
\{b_{d}(\widetilde{X}_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d},Z^{d}_{m_{j},1})\mathbbm{1}_{(\widetilde{\mathsf{A}}_{m_{j}}^{d})^{c}}\}_{j=1,\dots,4}
are independent conditionally on
\mathcal{F}
. Thus,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{4}b_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d},Z^{d}_{m_{j},1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\widetilde{\mathsf{A}}_{m_{j}}^{d}\right)^{c}}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]=\prod_{j=1}^{4}\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d},Z^{d}_{m_{j},1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\widetilde{\mathsf{A}}_{m_{j}}^{d}\right)^{c}}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]

(342)

\displaystyle\qquad=\prod_{j=1}^{4}\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d},Z^{d}_{m_{j},1}\right)\times\left(1-\exp\left(\sum_{i=1}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,i},Z_{m_{j},i}^{d}\right)\right)\right)_{+}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right],

(343)

by integrating the uniform variables
U_{m_{j}}

in (
340
).

Recalling the definition of
b_{d}
in (
286
), we can bound the expectation above with

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d},Z_{m_{j},1}^{d}\right)\left\{1-\exp\left(\sum_{i=1}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,i},Z_{m_{j},i}^{d}\right)\right)\right\}_{+}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]\right\rvert

(344)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{\sigma_{d}}{2}\sgn\left(\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}\left\{|\widetilde{X}_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d}|\geq\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2\right\}\right.\right.\right.

(345)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad-\left.\frac{1}{\sigma_{d}^{2v-1}r}\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,1}\mathbbm{1}\left\{|\widetilde{X}_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d}|<\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2\right\}\right)

(346)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.\left.\times\left\{1-\exp\left(\sum_{i=1}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,i},Z_{m_{j},i}^{d}\right)\right)\right\}_{+}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]\right|

(347)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[Z_{m_{j},1}^{d}\left\{1-\exp\left(\sum_{i=1}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,i},Z_{m_{j},i}^{d}\right)\right)\right\}_{+}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]\right\rvert.

(348)

For the first term, we use the boundedness of the
\sgn
function and of
\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,1}
in the set
\{|\widetilde{X}_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d}|\leq\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2\}
to obtain

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{\sigma_{d}}{2}\sgn\left(\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}\left\{|\widetilde{X}_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d}|\geq\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2\right\}-\frac{\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,1}}{\sigma_{d}^{2v-1}r}\mathbbm{1}\left\{|\widetilde{X}_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d}|<\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2\right\}\right)\right.\right.

(349)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.\left.\times\left\{1-\exp\left(\sum_{i=1}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,i},Z_{m_{j},i}^{d}\right)\right)\right\}_{+}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma_{d}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\left\{1-\exp\left(\sum_{i=1}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,i},Z_{m_{j},i}^{d}\right)\right)\right\}_{+}\right|\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]\leq\frac{\sigma_{d}}{2}.

(350)

We can write the second term as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[Z_{m_{j},1}^{d}\left(1-\exp\left(\sum_{i=1}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,i},Z^{d}_{m_{j},i}\right)\right)\right)_{+}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]

(351)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{G}\left(\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,1},\sum_{i=2}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,i},Z^{d}_{m_{j},i}\right)\right)\middle|\mathcal{F}\right],

(352)

where we define
\mathcal{G}(a,b)=\mathbb{E}\left[Z\left(1-\exp\left(\phi_{d}\left(a,Z\right)+b\right)\right)_{+}\right]
with
Z
a standard Gaussian. Because the function
x\mapsto(1-\exp(x))_{+}
is 1-Lipschitz, we have, using Cauchy-Schwarz and Lemma
3
in Appendix
D.2
,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[Z\left(1-\exp\left(\phi_{d}\left(a,Z\right)+b\right)\right)_{+}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[Z\left(1-\exp\left(b\right)\right)_{+}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[|Z|\left|\phi_{d}\left(a,Z\right)\right|\right]

(353)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[Z^{2}\right]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}\left(a,Z\right)^{2}\right]^{1/2}

(354)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}\left(a,Z\right)^{2}\right]^{1/2}

(355)

\displaystyle\leq Cd^{-\alpha}.

(356)

However,
\mathbb{E}\left[Z\left(1-\exp\left(b\right)\right)_{+}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[Z\right]\left(1-\exp\left(b\right)\right)_{+}=0
, and therefore

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{G}\left(\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,1},\sum_{i=2}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,i},Z^{d}_{m_{j},i}\right)\right)\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]\right|\leq Cd^{-\alpha}.

(357)

Combining equations (
344
), (
350
) and (
357
) and recalling that
\sigma_{d}=\ell d^{-\alpha}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d},Z_{m_{j},1}^{d}\right)\left\{1-\exp\left(\sum_{i=1}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,i},Z_{m_{j},i}^{d}\right)\right)\right\}_{+}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]\right\rvert\leq Cd^{-\alpha},

(358)

from which follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{(m_{1},\dots,m_{4})\in\mathcal{I}_{4}}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{i=1}^{4}b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{m_{i}-1,1},Z^{d}_{m_{i},1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\mathsf{A}^{d}_{m_{i}}\right)^{c}}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{(m_{1},\dots,m_{4})\in\mathcal{I}_{4}}\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{4}\frac{C}{d^{\alpha}}\right]

(359)

\displaystyle\leq\binom{k_{2}-k_{1}}{4}\frac{C}{d^{4\alpha}}\leq C\frac{(k_{2}-k_{1})^{4}}{d^{4\alpha}},

(360)

using that
|\mathcal{I}_{4}|=\binom{k_{2}-k_{1}}{4}
.

2.

If
(m_{1},..,m_{4})\in\mathcal{I}_{3}
, only three of the
m_{i}
s take distinct values; without loss of generality, we assume that
m_{1}
appears twice, while
m_{2},m_{3}
appear once. Proceeding as in case 1, we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{3}b_{d}\left(X_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d},Z^{d}_{m_{j},1}\right)^{1+\delta_{1,j}}\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\mathsf{A}_{m_{j}}^{d}\right)^{c}}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]\right|

(361)

\displaystyle=\prod_{j=1}^{3}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d},Z^{d}_{m_{j},1}\right)^{1+\delta_{1,j}}\left\{1-\exp\left(\sum_{i=1}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}^{d}_{m_{j}-1,i},Z_{m_{j},i}^{d}\right)\right)\right\}_{+}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]\right|,

(362)

where
\delta_{1,j}
denotes a Dirac’s delta.
For the terms
j\neq 1
, we use (
358
), while for the term
j=1
we bound the indicator function by 1 to obtain

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{3}b_{d}\left(X_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d},Z_{m_{j},1}^{d}\right)^{1+\delta_{1,j}}\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\mathsf{A}_{m_{j}}^{d}\right)^{c}}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]\right|

(363)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}_{m_{1}-1,1}^{d},Z_{m_{1},1}^{d}\right)^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]\right\rvert\prod_{j=2}^{3}\frac{C}{d^{\alpha}}

(364)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left(3+\frac{2\sigma_{d}^{2}}{2^{2}d^{2\alpha}}\right)\frac{C^{2}}{d^{2\alpha}}\leq C\frac{1}{d^{2\alpha}},

(365)

where the second-to-last inequality follows using the same approach taken
for (
336
) and recalling that
\sigma_{d}=\ell d^{-\alpha}
.
Hence,

\displaystyle\sum_{(m_{1},\dots,m_{4})\in\mathcal{I}_{3}}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{i=1}^{4}b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{m_{i}-1,1},Z^{d}_{m_{i},1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\mathsf{A}^{d}_{m_{i}}\right)^{c}}\right]\right|

(366)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C\binom{k_{2}-k_{1}}{3}\frac{1}{d^{2\alpha}}\leq C\frac{(k_{2}-k_{1})^{3}}{d^{2\alpha}}.

(367)

3.

If
(m_{1},..,m_{4})\in\mathcal{I}_{2}
, we have two different cases: the

m_{i}
s take the two values twice or three
m_{i}
s have the same value.
For the first one we assume, without loss of generality, that
m_{1},m_{2}
take distinct values and appear each twice. Bounding the indicator function with 1,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{2}b_{d}\left(X_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d},Z_{m_{j},1}^{d}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\mathsf{A}_{m_{j}}^{d}\right)^{c}}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]\right]

(368)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d},Z_{m_{j},1}^{d}\right)^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]\right].

(369)

Since, conditionally on
\mathcal{F}
, the random variables inside the expectation are normals with bounded mean and variance
1
, we have, using the same approach taken for (
336
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d},Z_{m_{j},1}^{d}\right)^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]\right]\leq\left(1+\frac{2\sigma_{d}^{2}}{2^{2}}\right)^{2}\leq C.

(370)

For the second case we assume, without loss of generality, that
m_{1}
appears three times while
m_{2}
appears once. Following a similar approach to case 2, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{2}b_{d}\left(X_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d},Z_{m_{j},1}^{d}\right)^{1+2\delta_{1,j}}\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\mathsf{A}_{m_{j}}^{d}\right)^{c}}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]\right]\right|

(371)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{2}\left|b_{d}\left(\widetilde{X}_{m_{j}-1,1}^{d},Z_{m_{j},1}^{d}\right)\right|^{1+2\delta_{1,j}}\middle|\mathcal{F}\right]\right]\leq C,

(372)

where
\delta_{1,j}
denotes a Dirac’s delta.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{(m_{1},\dots,m_{4})\in\mathcal{I}_{2}}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{i=1}^{4}\left\{b_{d}(X^{d}_{m_{i}-1,1},Z^{d}_{m_{i},1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\mathsf{A}^{d}_{m_{i}}\right)^{c}}\right]\right|

(373)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C\left(\binom{4}{2}+\binom{4}{3}\right)\binom{k_{2}-k_{1}}{2}\leq C(k_{2}-k_{1})^{2}.

(374)

4.

If
(m_{1},..,m_{4})\in\mathcal{I}_{1}
(i.e., all
m_{i}
s take the same value), we bound the indicator function by 1 and, using the same approach taken for (
336
), we find

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{m_{1}-1,1},Z^{d}_{m_{1},1}\right)^{4}\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\mathsf{A}^{d}_{m_{1}}\right)^{c}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\left(3+\frac{2\sigma_{d}^{4}}{2^{4}}\right)\leq C,

(375)

since
\sigma_{d}=\ell d^{-\alpha}
and
d\in\mathbb{N}
.
Hence,

\displaystyle\sum_{(m_{1},\dots,m_{4})\in\mathcal{I}_{1}}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{i=1}^{4}b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{m_{1}-1,1},Z^{d}_{m_{1},1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\mathsf{A}^{d}_{m_{i}}\right)^{c}}\right]\right|\leq C\binom{k_{2}-k_{1}}{1}=C(k_{2}-k_{1}).

(376)

The result follows combining (
360
), (
366
), (
373
) and (
376
) in (
337
).

∎

C.3
Proof of Proposition
2

We start by proving the following lemma.

Lemma 2
.

Let
\nu
be a limit point of the sequence of laws
(\nu_{d})_{d\geq 1}
of
\{(L_{t}^{d})_{t\geq 0}\,:\,d\in\mathbb{N}^{*}\}
. Then for any
t\geq 0
, the pushforward measure of
\nu
by
W_{t}
is
\pi^{\mathrm{L}}(\mathrm{d}x)=\exp(-|x|)\mathrm{d}x/2
.

Proof.

Using (
304
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|L_{t}^{d}-X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}^{d}\right|\right]

(377)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(d^{2\alpha}t-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor)\left[\sigma_{d}Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil,1}-\frac{\sigma^{2}_{d}}{2}\sgn(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1})\right]\mathbbm{1}\left\{|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}|\geq\sigma^{2v}_{d}r/2\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil}}\right|\right]

(378)

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert(d^{2\alpha}t-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor)\left[\sigma_{d}Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil,1}-\frac{1}{\sigma_{d}^{2(v-1)}r}X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}\right]\mathbbm{1}\left\{|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}|<\sigma^{2v}_{d}r/2\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil}}\right\rvert\right]

(379)

\displaystyle\leq(d^{2\alpha}t-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor)\left(\sigma_{d}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil,1}\right\rvert\right]+\frac{\sigma_{d}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert\sgn(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1})\right\rvert\right]\right.

(380)

\displaystyle\left.\qquad+\frac{1}{\sigma_{d}^{2(v-1)}r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}\right\rvert\mathbbm{1}\left\{|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}|<\sigma^{2v}_{d}r/2\right\}\right]\right)

(381)

\displaystyle\leq(d^{2\alpha}t-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor)\left(\frac{\ell}{d^{\alpha}}\mathbb{E}\left[(Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil,1})^{2}\right]^{1/2}+\frac{\ell^{2}}{2d^{2\alpha}}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{d}^{2(v-1)}r}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\sigma^{2v}_{d}r}{2}\right]\right)

(382)

\displaystyle\leq(d^{2\alpha}t-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor)\left(\frac{\ell}{d^{\alpha}}+\frac{\ell^{2}}{2d^{2\alpha}}+\frac{\ell^{2}}{2d^{2\alpha}}\right)\leq\frac{C}{d^{\alpha}},

(383)

where we used Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and the fact that the moments of
Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil,1}
are bounded.
The above guarantees that,

\lim_{d\rightarrow\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|L_{t}^{d}-X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}^{d}\right|\right]=0.

(384)

As
(\nu_{d})_{d\geq 1}
converges weakly towards
\nu
, for any Lipschitz bounded function
\psi:\real\to\real
,

\lim_{d\rightarrow\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\psi\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)\right]=\lim_{d\rightarrow\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\psi\left(L_{t}^{d}\right)\right]=\mathbb{E}^{\nu}\left[\psi(W_{t})\right].

(385)

The result follows since
X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}^{d}
is distributed according to
\pi^{\mathrm{L}}(\mathrm{d}x)=\exp(-|x|)\mathrm{d}x/2
for any
t\geq 0
and
d\in\mathbb{N}
.
∎

We are now ready to prove Proposition
2
:

Proof of Proposition
2
.

Let
\nu
be a limit point of
(\nu_{d})_{d\geq 1}
. We start by showing that if for any
V\in\mathrm{C}^{\infty}_{\mathrm{c}}(\real,\real)
,
m\in\mathbb{N}
, any bounded and continuous mapping
\rho:\real^{m}\rightarrow\real
and any
0\leq t_{1}\leq\dots\leq t_{m}\leq s\leq t
,
\nu
satisfies

\mathbb{E}^{\nu}\left[\left(V(W_{t})-V(W_{s})-\int_{s}^{t}\mathrm{L}V(W_{u})\mathrm{d}u\right)\rho(W_{t_{1}},\dots,W_{t_{m}})\right]=0,

(386)

then
\nu
is a solution to the martingale problem associated with
\mathrm{L}
.

Let
\mathfrak{F}_{s}
denote the
\sigma
-algebra generated by

\left\{\rho(W_{t_{1}},\dots,W_{t_{m}})\,:\,m\in\mathbb{N},\ \rho:\real^{m}\rightarrow\real\textrm{ bounded and continuous, and }0\leq t_{1}\leq\dots\leq t_{m}\leq s\right\}.

(387)

Then,

\mathbb{E}^{\nu}\left[V(W_{t})-V(W_{s})-\int_{s}^{t}\mathrm{L}V(W_{u})\mathrm{d}u\middle|\mathfrak{F}_{s}\right]=0,

(388)

showing that the process

\left(V(W_{t})-V(W_{0})-\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{L}V(W_{u})\mathrm{d}u\right)_{t\geq 0}

(389)

is a martingale w.r.t.
\nu
and the filtration
(\mathfrak{F}_{t})_{t\geq 0}
.

To prove (
386
), it is enough to show that for any
V\in\mathrm{C}^{\infty}_{\mathrm{c}}(\real,\real)
,
m\in\mathbb{N}
and any bounded and continuous mapping
\rho:\real^{m}\rightarrow\real
and any
0\leq t_{1}\leq\dots\leq t_{m}\leq s\leq t
, the mapping

\Psi_{s,t}:w\longmapsto\left(V(w_{t})-V(w_{s})-\int_{s}^{t}\mathrm{L}V(w_{u})\mathrm{d}u\right)\rho\left(w_{t_{1}},\dots,w_{t_{m}}\right),

(390)

is continuous on a
\nu
-almost sure subset of
\mathrm{C}(\mathbb{R}_{+},\real)
.
Let

\bm{\mathrm{W}}=\left\{w\in\mathrm{C}(\mathbb{R}_{+},\real)\,:\,w_{u}\neq 0\textrm{ for almost any }u\in[s,t]\right\}.

(391)

Since
w\in\bm{\mathrm{W}}^{c}
if and only if
\int_{s}^{t}\mathbbm{1}_{\{0\}}(w_{u})\mathrm{d}u>0
, using Lemma
2
and the Fubini–Tonelli’s theorem,

\mathbb{E}^{\nu}\left[\int_{s}^{t}\mathbbm{1}_{\{0\}}(W_{u})\mathrm{d}u\right]=\int_{s}^{t}\mathbb{E}^{\nu}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{0\}}(W_{u})\right]\mathrm{d}u=\int_{s}^{t}\pi^{\mathrm{L}}(\{0\})\mathrm{d}u=0,

(392)

and we have that
\nu(\bm{\mathrm{W}}^{c})=0
.

Since
w\mapsto w_{u}
is continuous for any
u\geq 0
, so are
w\mapsto V(w_{u})
and
w\mapsto\rho(w_{t_{1}},\dots,w_{t_{m}})
. Thus, it is enough to prove that the mapping
w\mapsto\int_{s}^{t}\mathrm{L}V(w_{u})\mathrm{d}u
is continuous. Let
(w^{n})_{n\geq 0}
be a sequence in
\mathrm{C}(\mathbb{R}_{+},\real)
that converges to
w\in\bm{\mathrm{W}}
in the uniform topology on compact sets. Let
u
be such that
w_{u}\neq 0
, therefore, since the
\sgn
function is continuous in a neighbourhood of
w_{u}
,

\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathrm{L}V(w^{n}_{u})=\mathrm{L}V(w_{u})
, thus
\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathrm{L}V(w^{n}_{u})=\mathrm{L}V(w_{u})
for almost any
u\in[s,t]
.
Finally, using the boundedness of the sequence
(\mathrm{L}V(w^{n}_{u}))_{n\geq 0}
and Lebesgue’s dominated convergence theorem,

\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\int_{s}^{t}\mathrm{L}V(w^{n}_{u})\mathrm{d}u=\int_{s}^{t}\mathrm{L}V(w_{u})\mathrm{d}u,

(393)

which proves that the mappings
\Psi_{s,t}
are continuous on
\bm{\mathrm{W}}
.
∎

C.4
Proof of Theorem
3

Let us introduce, for any
n\in\mathbb{N}
,
\mathcal{F}_{n,1}^{d}=\sigma(\{X_{k,1}^{d},0\leq k\leq n\})
, the
\sigma
-algebra generated by the first components of
\{X^{d}_{k}\mid 0\leq k\leq n\}
. We also introduce for any
V\in\mathrm{C}^{\infty}_{\mathrm{c}}(\real,\real)

\displaystyle M_{n}^{d}(V)

\displaystyle=\frac{\ell}{d^{\alpha}}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}V^{\prime}(X_{k,1}^{d})

(394)

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{k,1},Z^{d}_{k+1,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{k+1}^{d}}-\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{k,1},Z^{d}_{k+1,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{k+1}^{d}}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{k,1}^{d}\right]\right)

(395)

\displaystyle+\frac{\ell^{2}}{2d^{2\alpha}}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}V^{\prime\prime}(X_{k,1}^{d})

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{k,1},Z^{d}_{k+1,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{k+1}^{d}}-\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{k,1},Z^{d}_{k+1,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{k+1}^{d}}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{k,1}^{d}\right]\right).

(396)

where
b_{d}
is defined in (
286
).

The proof of Theorem
3
follows using the sufficient condition in Proposition
2
, the tightness of the sequence
(\nu_{d})_{d\geq 1}
established in Proposition
1
and Proposition
15
below.

Proof.

Using Proposition
1
, Proposition
2
and Proposition
15
below, it is enough to show that for any
V\in\mathrm{C}_{\mathrm{c}}^{\infty}(\real,\real),m\geq 1
, any
0\leq t_{1}\leq\cdots\leq t_{m}\leq s\leq t
and any bounded and continuous mapping
\rho:\real^{m}\to\real
,

\lim_{d\rightarrow\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(M^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil}(V)-M^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil}(V)\right)\rho(L_{t_{1}}^{d},...,L_{t_{m}}^{d})\right]=0,

(397)

where, for any
n\geq 1
,
M_{n}^{d}(V)
is given by (
395
). However, this is straightforwardly obtained by taking successively the conditional expectations with respect to
\mathcal{F}^{d}_{k,1}
for
k=\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil,\dots,\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil
.
∎

Proposition 15
.

For any
0\leq s\leq t
,
V\in\mathrm{C}_{\mathrm{c}}(\real,\real)
we have

\lim_{d\rightarrow\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|V\left(L_{t}^{d}\right)-V\left(L_{s}^{d}\right)-\int_{s}^{t}\mathrm{L}V\left(L_{u}^{d}\right)\mathrm{d}u-\left(M_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil}^{d}\left(V\right)-M_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil}^{d}\left(V\right)\right)\right|\right]=0,

(398)

where
(L_{t}^{d})_{t\geq 0}
is defined in (
15
).

Proof.

The process
(L^{d}_{t})_{t\geq 0}
is piecewise linear, thus it has finite variation. For any
\tau\geq 0
, we define

\mathrm{d}L_{\tau}^{d}=d^{2\alpha}\sigma_{d}b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\mathrm{d}\tau.

(399)

Since
\sigma_{d}=\ell d^{-\alpha}
with
\alpha=1/3
and using the fundamental theorem of calculus for piecewise
C^{1}
maps

\displaystyle V\left(L_{t}^{d}\right)-V\left(L_{s}^{d}\right)=\ell d^{\alpha}\int_{s}^{t}V^{\prime}\left(L_{\tau}^{d}\right)b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\mathrm{d}\tau,

(400)

where
b_{d}
is defined in (
286
).
A Taylor expansion of
V^{\prime}
with Lagrange remainder about
X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}
gives

\displaystyle V^{\prime}\left(L^{d}_{\tau}\right)

\displaystyle=V^{\prime}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right)

(401)

\displaystyle+\frac{\ell}{d^{\alpha}}\left(d^{2\alpha}\tau-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor\right)V^{\prime\prime}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right)b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}

(402)

\displaystyle+\frac{\ell^{2}}{2d^{2\alpha}}\left(d^{2\alpha}\tau-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor\right)^{2}V^{(3)}\left(\chi_{\tau}\right)b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}},

(403)

where for any point
\tau\in[s,t]
, there exists
\chi_{\tau}\in[X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},L_{\tau}^{d}]
.
Substituting the above into (
400
) we obtain

\displaystyle V\left(L_{t}^{d}\right)-V\left(L_{s}^{d}\right)=\ell d^{\alpha}\int_{s}^{t}V^{\prime}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right)b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\mathrm{d}\tau

(404)

\displaystyle\qquad+\ell^{2}\int_{s}^{t}\left(d^{2\alpha}\tau-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor\right)V^{\prime\prime}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right)b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\mathrm{d}\tau

(405)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\ell^{3}}{2d^{\alpha}}\int_{s}^{t}\left(d^{2\alpha}\tau-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor\right)^{2}V^{(3)}\left(\chi_{\tau}\right)b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)^{3}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\mathrm{d}\tau.

(406)

Since
V^{(3)}
is bounded, using Fubini-Tonelli’s theorem and recalling the definition of
b_{d}
in (
286
), we have that

\displaystyle\frac{\ell^{3}}{2d^{\alpha}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert\int_{s}^{t}\left(d^{2\alpha}\tau-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor\right)^{2}V^{(3)}\left(\chi_{\tau}\right)b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)^{3}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\mathrm{d}\tau\right\rvert\right]

(407)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C\frac{\ell^{3}}{2d^{\alpha}}\int_{s}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\lvert Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right\rvert+\frac{\ell}{2d^{\alpha}}\right)^{3}\right]\mathrm{d}\tau\underset{d\to\infty}{\longrightarrow}0,

(408)

since the moments of
Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}
are bounded.

For the second term in (
405
), we observe that most of the integrand is piecewise constant since the process
X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}
evolves in discrete time. Then, for any integer
d^{2\alpha}s\leq k\leq d^{2\alpha}t-1
,

\displaystyle\int_{k/d^{2\alpha}}^{(k+1)/d^{2\alpha}}\left(d^{2\alpha}\tau-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor\right)V^{\prime\prime}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right)b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\mathrm{d}\tau

(409)

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{2d^{2\alpha}}V^{\prime\prime}\left(X^{d}_{k,1}\right)b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{k,1},Z^{d}_{k+1,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{k+1}^{d}}

(410)

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{2}\int_{k/d^{2\alpha}}^{(k+1)/d^{2\alpha}}V^{\prime\prime}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right)b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\mathrm{d}\tau.

(411)

Thus, we can write

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{t}\left(d^{2\alpha}\tau-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor\right)V^{\prime\prime}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right)b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\mathrm{d}\tau

(412)

\displaystyle=I_{1}+I_{2},

(413)

where we define

I_{2}:=\frac{1}{2}\int_{s}^{t}V^{\prime\prime}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right)b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\mathrm{d}\tau,

(414)

and

I_{1}:=\left[\int_{s}^{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil/d^{2\alpha}}+\int_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor/d^{2\alpha}}^{t}\right]\left(d^{2\alpha}\tau-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor-\frac{1}{2}\right)V^{\prime\prime}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right)\\
\times b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\mathrm{d}\tau.

(415)

In addition, we have

I_{1}=\frac{1}{2d^{2\alpha}}\left(d^{2\alpha}s-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}s\rfloor\right)\left(\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil-d^{2\alpha}s\right)V^{\prime\prime}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}s\rfloor,1}\right)b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}s\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil}^{d}}\\
+\frac{1}{2d^{2\alpha}}\left(d^{2\alpha}t-\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor\right)\left(\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil-d^{2\alpha}t\right)V^{\prime\prime}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}\right)b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil}^{d}},

(416)

and, since
V^{\prime\prime}
and the moments of
Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil,1}
are bounded,
\lim_{d\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|I_{1}\right|\right]=0
. Thus,

\lim_{d\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|V\left(L_{t}^{d}\right)-V\left(L_{s}^{d}\right)-I_{s,t}\right|\right]=0,

(417)

where

\displaystyle I_{s,t}

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{t}\left\{\ell d^{\alpha}V^{\prime}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right)b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)\right.

(418)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left.\frac{\ell^{2}}{2}V^{\prime\prime}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right)b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\right\}\mathrm{d}\tau.

(419)

Next, we use (
30
) and write

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}\mathrm{L}V\left(L^{d}_{\tau}\right)\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{t}\frac{h^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell)}{2}\left[V^{\prime\prime}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor 1}^{d}\right)-\sgn\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)V^{\prime}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)\right]\mathrm{d}\tau-T_{3}^{d},

(420)

where we define

T_{3}^{d}=\int_{s}^{t}\left(\mathrm{L}V\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)-\mathrm{L}V\left(L^{d}_{\tau}\right)\right)\mathrm{d}\tau.

(421)

Finally, we write the difference
M^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil}(V)-M^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil}(V)
as the integral of a piecewise constant function

\displaystyle M^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil}(V)-M^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil}(V)=I_{s,t}

(422)

\displaystyle\qquad-\int_{s}^{t}\left(\ell d^{\alpha}V^{\prime}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right]\right.

(423)

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+\frac{\ell^{2}}{2}V^{\prime\prime}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right]\right)\mathrm{d}\tau

(424)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-T_{4}^{d}-T_{5}^{d},

where
T_{4}^{d}
and
T_{5}^{d}
account for the difference between the sum in (
395
) and the integral, and are defined as

\displaystyle T_{4}^{d}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\ell}{d^{\alpha}}\left(\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil-d^{2\alpha}t\right)V^{\prime}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)\left\{b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil}^{d}}\right.

(425)

\displaystyle\qquad\left.-\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil}^{d}}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}^{d}\right]\right\}

(426)

\displaystyle-\frac{\ell^{2}}{2d^{2\alpha}}\left(\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil-d^{2\alpha}t\right)V^{\prime\prime}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)\left\{b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil}^{d}}\right.

(427)

\displaystyle\qquad\left.-\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil}^{d}}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}t\rfloor,1}^{d}\right]\right\},

(428)

and
T_{5}^{d}=-T_{4}^{d}
with
t
substituted by
s
.
Putting (
418
), (
420
) and (
422
) together we obtain

I_{s,t}-\int_{s}^{t}\mathrm{L}V\left(L^{d}_{\tau}\right)\mathrm{d}\tau-\left(M^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}t\rceil}(V)-M^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}s\rceil}(V)\right)=T_{1}^{d}+T_{2}^{d}+T_{3}^{d}+T_{4}^{d}+T_{5}^{d},

(429)

where
T_{1}^{d}
takes into account all the terms involving
V^{\prime}(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d})
, and
T_{2}^{d}
the terms involving
V^{\prime\prime}(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d})
:

\displaystyle T_{1}^{d}

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{t}V^{\prime}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)

(430)

\displaystyle\times\left\{\ell d^{\alpha}\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right]+\frac{h^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell)}{2}\sgn\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)\right\}\mathrm{d}\tau,

(431)

\displaystyle T_{2}^{d}

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{t}V^{\prime\prime}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)

(432)

\displaystyle\times\left\{\frac{\ell^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right]-\frac{h^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell)}{2}\right\}\mathrm{d}\tau.

(433)

To obtain (
398
) it is then sufficient to prove that for any
1\leq i\leq 5
,
\lim_{d\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|T_{i}^{d}\right|\right]=0
.

Since
V^{\prime},V^{\prime\prime}
are bounded and
b_{d}
is bounded in expectation because the moments of
Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}
are bounded, it is easy to show that
\lim_{d\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|T_{i}^{d}\right|\right]=0
for
i=4,5
.
For
T_{3}^{d}
, we write
T_{3}^{d}=h^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell)(T_{3,1}^{d}-T_{3,2}^{d})/2
, where

\displaystyle T_{3,1}^{d}

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{t}\left\{V^{\prime\prime}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)-V^{\prime\prime}\left(L_{\tau}^{d}\right)\right\}\mathrm{d}\tau,

(434)

\displaystyle T_{3,2}^{d}

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{t}\left\{\sgn\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)V^{\prime}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)-\sgn\left(L_{\tau}^{d}\right)V^{\prime}\left(L_{\tau}^{d}\right)\right\}\mathrm{d}\tau.

(435)

Using Fubini-Tonelli’s theorem, the convergence of
X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}
to
L_{\tau}^{d}
in Lemma
2
and Lebesgue’s dominated convergence theorem we obtain

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|T_{3,1}^{d}\right|\right]\leq\int_{s}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|V^{\prime\prime}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)-V^{\prime\prime}\left(L_{\tau}^{d}\right)\right|\right]\mathrm{d}\tau\underset{d\to\infty}{\longrightarrow}0.

(436)

We can further decompose
T_{3,2}^{d}
as

T_{3,2}^{d}=\int_{s}^{t}\left\{\sgn\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)-\sgn\left(L_{\tau}^{d}\right)\right\}V^{\prime}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)\mathrm{d}\tau\\
+\int_{s}^{t}\sgn\left(L_{\tau}^{d}\right)\left\{V^{\prime}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)-V^{\prime}\left(L_{\tau}^{d}\right)\right\}\mathrm{d}\tau.

(437)

Proceeding as for
T_{3,1}^{d}
, it is easy to show that the second integral converges to 0 as
d\to\infty
.
We then bound the first integral by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int_{s}^{t}\left\{\sgn\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)-\sgn\left(L_{\tau}^{d}\right)\right\}V^{\prime}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)\mathrm{d}\tau\right|\right]

(438)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C\int_{s}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sgn\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)-\sgn\left(L_{\tau}^{d}\right)\right|\right]\mathrm{d}\tau.

(439)

However, since
\{\sgn(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d})\neq\sgn(L_{\tau}^{d})\}\subset\{\sgn(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d})\neq\sgn(X_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}^{d})\}
, using Lemma
4
in Appendix
D.3
we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sgn\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)-\sgn\left(L_{\tau}^{d}\right)\right|\right]

\displaystyle=2\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left\{\sgn\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)\neq\sgn\left(L_{\tau}^{d}\right)\right\}\right]

(440)

\displaystyle=2\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left\{\sgn\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)\neq\sgn\left(X_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}^{d}\right)\right\}\right]\underset{d\to\infty}{\longrightarrow}0.

(441)

The above and the dominated converge theorem show that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int_{s}^{t}\left\{\sgn\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)-\sgn\left(L_{\tau}^{d}\right)\right\}V^{\prime}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)\mathrm{d}\tau\right|\right]\underset{d\to\infty}{\longrightarrow}0.

(442)

Consider then
T_{1}^{d}
, recalling that the derivatives of
V
are bounded, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|T_{1}^{d}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\int_{s}^{t}C\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert\ell d^{\alpha}\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right]\right.\right.

(443)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left.\left.\frac{h^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell)}{2}\sgn\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right)\right\rvert\right]\mathrm{d}\tau

(444)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{s}^{t}C\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\left|D_{1,\tau}^{(1)}\right|\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left|D_{2,\tau}^{(1)}\right|\right]\right\}\mathrm{d}\tau,

where we define

\displaystyle D_{1,\tau}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\ell d^{\alpha}\mathbb{E}\left[Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}}\middle|\mathcal{F}^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right],

(445)

\displaystyle D_{2,\tau}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{h^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell)}{2}\sgn\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right)

\displaystyle-\ell d^{\alpha}\left(\frac{\sigma_{d}}{2}\sgn(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1})\mathbbm{1}_{|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}|\geq\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{d}^{2v-1}r}X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\mathbbm{1}_{|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}|<\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}}\middle|\mathcal{F}^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right].

Let us start with
D_{1,\tau}^{(1)}
:

D_{1,\tau}^{(1)}=\ell d^{\alpha}\mathbb{E}\left[Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\left(1\wedge\exp\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}\right)\right\}\right)\middle|\mathcal{F}^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right],

(446)

where
\phi_{d}
is given in (
287
).
Then, by independence of the components of
Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}
, we have

\mathbb{E}\left[Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\left(1\wedge\exp\left\{\sum_{i=2}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}\right)\right\}\right)\middle|\mathcal{F}^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right]\\
=\mathbb{E}\left[Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[1\wedge\exp\left\{\sum_{i=2}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}\right)\right\}\middle|\mathcal{F}^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right]=0.

(447)

This allows us to write

\mathbb{E}\left[|D_{1,\tau}^{(1)}|\right]\leq\ell d^{\alpha}\mathbb{E}\left[|Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}|\right.\\
\left.\left\lvert 1\wedge\exp\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}\right)\right\}-1\wedge\exp\left\{\sum_{i=2}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}\right)\right\}\right\rvert\right].

(448)

However,
x\mapsto 1\wedge\exp(x)
is a 1-Lipschitz function, thus

\mathbb{E}\left[|D_{1,\tau}^{(1)}|\right]\leq\ell d^{\alpha}\mathbb{E}\left[|Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}|\left\lvert\phi_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)\right\rvert\right],

(449)

and
D_{1,\tau}^{(1)}\to 0
as
d\to\infty
by Lemma
5
in Appendix
D.3
.

For
D_{2,\tau}^{(1)}
, we observe that

\displaystyle-\frac{\sigma_{d}}{2}\mathbbm{1}_{|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}|<\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2}\leq\frac{1}{\sigma^{2v-1}r}X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\mathbbm{1}_{|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}|<\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2}\leq\frac{\sigma_{d}}{2}\mathbbm{1}_{|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}|<\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2}.

(450)

Distinguishing between
X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}<0
and
X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\geq 0
, it follows that

\displaystyle|D_{2,\tau}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\leq\left\lvert\sgn\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right)\right\rvert

(451)

\displaystyle\times\left|\frac{h^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell)}{2}-\ell d^{\alpha}\left(\frac{\sigma_{d}}{2}\mathbbm{1}_{|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}|\geq\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2}+\frac{\sigma_{d}}{2}\mathbbm{1}_{|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}|<\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}}\middle|\mathcal{F}^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right]\right\rvert

(452)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\lvert h^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell)-\ell^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}}\middle|\mathcal{F}^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}r\rfloor,1\rfloor}\right]\right\rvert,

(453)

where we recall that
\sigma_{d}=\ell d^{-\alpha}
with
\alpha=1/3
.
Using the triangle inequality we obtain

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}\left[|D_{2,\tau}^{(1)}|\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert h^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell)-\ell^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[1\wedge\exp\left(\sum_{i=1}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}^{d}\right)\right)\middle|\mathcal{F}^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right]\right\rvert\right]

(454)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert h^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell)-\ell^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[1\wedge\exp\left(\sum_{i=2}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}^{d}\right)\right)\middle|\mathcal{F}^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right]\right\rvert\right]

(455)

\displaystyle+\ell^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert 1\wedge\exp\left(\sum_{i=2}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}^{d}\right)\right)-1\wedge\exp\left(\sum_{i=1}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}^{d}\right)\right)\right\rvert\right],

(456)

where we used Jensen’s inequality to remove the conditional expectation in the last term.
Recalling that
x\mapsto 1\wedge\exp(x)
is 1-Lipschitz, we can then bound the second term

\displaystyle\ell^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert 1\wedge\exp\left(\sum_{i=2}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}^{d}\right)\right)-1\wedge\exp\left(\sum_{i=1}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}^{d}\right)\right)\right\rvert\right]

(457)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\ell^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}^{d}\right)\right|\right],

(458)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\ell^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}^{d}\right)^{2}\right]^{1/2},

(459)

where the final expectation converges to zero as
d\to\infty
by Proposition
17
.
For the remaining term in
D_{2,\tau}^{(1)}
, since
(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i},Z^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i})_{2\leq i\leq n}
is independent of
\mathcal{F}^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}
, we have

\displaystyle\ell^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[1\wedge\exp\left(\sum_{i=2}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}^{d}\right)\right)\middle|\mathcal{F}^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right]

(460)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\ell^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[1\wedge\exp\left(\sum_{i=2}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}^{d}\right)\right)\right],

(461)

and, using again the fact that
x\mapsto 1\wedge\exp(x)
is 1-Lipschitz, we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert h^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell)-\ell^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[1\wedge\exp\left(\sum_{i=2}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}^{d}\right)\right)\right]\right\rvert

(462)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left\lvert h^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell)-\ell^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[1\wedge\exp\left(\sum_{i=1}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}^{d}\right]\right)\right)\right\rvert

(463)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\ell^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}^{d}\right)\right|\right].

(464)

The last term goes to 0 as shown in (
458
), and, as
h^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell)=\ell^{2}a^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell)
, with

a^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell)=\lim_{d\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[1\wedge\exp\left(\sum_{i=1}^{d}\phi_{d,i}\right)\right],

(465)

by Theorem
2
, we obtain

\lim_{d\to\infty}\left\lvert h^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell)-\ell^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[1\wedge\exp\left(\sum_{i=2}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}^{d}\right)\right)\right\rvert\right]=0,

(466)

showing that
D_{2,\tau}^{(1)}\to 0
as
d\to\infty
.
To obtain convergence of
T_{1}^{d}
, we observe that for any
\tau\in[s,t]
,

D_{1,\tau}^{(1)}
and
D_{2,\tau}^{(1)}
follow the same distributions as

D_{1,s}^{(1)}
and
D_{2,s}^{(1)}
, since for any
k\in\mathbb{N}
,
X_{k}^{d}

has distribution
\pi^{\mathrm{L}}_{d}
. Therefore, the convergence towards zero of

\mathbb{E}[|D_{1,\tau}^{(1)}|]
and
\mathbb{E}[|D_{2,\tau}^{(1)}|]

is uniform for
\tau\in[s,t]
, which gives us
T_{1}^{d}\to 0
as
d\to\infty
.

Finally, consider
T_{2}^{d}
. Using analogous arguments to those used for
T_{1}^{d}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[|T_{2}^{d}|\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{s}^{t}\frac{\ell^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[b_{d}\left(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1},Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}^{d}}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d}\right]-a^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell,r)\right\rvert\right]\mathrm{d}\tau

(467)

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{s}^{t}\frac{\ell^{2}}{2}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[|D_{1,\tau}^{(2)}|\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[|D_{2,\tau}^{(2)}|\right]\mathbb{E}\left[|D_{3,\tau}^{(2)}|\right]\right\}\mathrm{d}\tau,

where we define

\displaystyle D_{1,\tau}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}}\middle|\mathcal{F}^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right]-a^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell,r),

(468)

\displaystyle D_{2,\tau}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\sigma_{d}}{2}\sgn(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1})\mathbbm{1}\{|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}|\geq\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2\}\right.

(469)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad+\left.\frac{1}{\sigma_{d}^{2v-1}r}X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\mathbbm{1}\{|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}|<\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2\}\right)^{2}\times\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}}\middle|\mathcal{F}^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right],

\displaystyle D_{3,\tau}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=2\left(\frac{\sigma_{d}}{2}\sgn(X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1})\mathbbm{1}\{|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}|\geq\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2\}\right.

(470)

\displaystyle\left.\qquad+\frac{1}{\sigma_{d}^{2v-1}r}X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\mathbbm{1}\{|X^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}|<\sigma_{d}^{2v}r/2\}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}}\middle|\mathcal{F}^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right].

Using (
450
), Cauchy-Schwarz’s inequality and the fact that the moments of
Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}
are bounded we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[|D_{2,\tau}^{(2)}|\right]\leq\frac{\sigma_{d}^{2}}{4}\underset{d\to\infty}{\longrightarrow}0,\qquad\mathbb{E}\left[|D_{3,\tau}^{(2)}|\right]\leq C\sigma_{d}\underset{d\to\infty}{\longrightarrow}0,

(471)

since
\sigma_{d}=\ell d^{-\alpha}
with
\alpha=1/3
.
The remaining term is bounded similarly to
D_{2,\tau}^{(1)}
, using the fact that
x\mapsto 1\wedge\exp(x)
is 1-Lipschitz, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[|D_{3,\tau}^{(2)}|\right]

(472)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\left(1\wedge\exp\left(\sum_{i=2}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}^{d}\right)\right)\right)\middle|\mathcal{F}^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right]-a^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell,r)\right\rvert\right]

(473)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\left|\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}^{d}\right)\right\rvert\right].

(474)

The second expectation is bounded as (
458
) using Cauchy-Schwarz’s inequality and Proposition
17
. For the first expectation, we use the conditional independence of the components of
Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil}
and write

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\left(1\wedge\exp\left(\sum_{i=2}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}^{d}\right)\right)\right)\middle|\mathcal{F}^{d}_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,1}\right]

(475)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(Z^{d}_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,1}\right)^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1\wedge\exp\left(\sum_{i=2}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}^{d}\right)\right)\right)\right]

(476)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1\wedge\exp\left(\sum_{i=2}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}^{d}\right)\right)\right)\right].

(477)

It follows that
\mathbb{E}[|D_{3,\tau}^{(2)}|]\to 0
as
d\to\infty
since, by Theorem
2
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1\wedge\exp\left(\sum_{i=2}^{d}\phi_{d}\left(X_{\lfloor d^{2\alpha}\tau\rfloor,i}^{d},Z_{\lceil d^{2\alpha}\tau\rceil,i}^{d}\right)\right)\right)\right]-a^{\mathrm{L}}(\ell,r)\right\rvert\to 0.

(478)

Combining the results for
T_{i}^{d}
,
i=1,\dots,5
we obtain the result.
∎

Appendix D
Moments and integrals for the Laplace distribution

D.1
Moments of acceptance ratio for the Laplace distribution

The indicator functions in the definition of
\phi_{d}
identify four different regions:

\displaystyle R_{1}

\displaystyle:=\left\{(x,z):|x|<\sigma^{2v}r/2\wedge\left\lvert\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)x+\sigma z\right\rvert<\sigma^{2v}r/2\right\},

(479)

\displaystyle R_{2}

\displaystyle:=\left\{(x,z):|x|\geq\sigma^{2v}r/2\wedge\left\lvert x-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sgn(x)+\sigma z\right\rvert<\sigma^{2v}r/2\right\},

(480)

\displaystyle R_{3}

\displaystyle:=\left\{(x,z):|x|<\sigma^{2v}r/2\wedge\left\lvert\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)x+\sigma z\right\rvert\geq\sigma^{2v}r/2\right\},

(481)

\displaystyle R_{4}

\displaystyle:=\left\{(x,z):|x|>\sigma^{2v}r/2\wedge\left\lvert x-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sgn(x)+\sigma z\right\rvert>\sigma^{2v}r/2\right\},

(482)

with corresponding acceptance ratios

\displaystyle\phi_{d}^{1}(x,z)

\displaystyle=|x|-\left\lvert\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)x+\sigma z\right\rvert+\frac{z^{2}}{2}

(483)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\left(\frac{2}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}-\frac{1}{\sigma^{4(m-1)}r^{2}}\right)x-\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)\sigma z\right)^{2}

(484)

\displaystyle\phi_{d}^{2}(x,z)

\displaystyle=|x|-\left\lvert x-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sgn(x)+\sigma z\right\rvert+\frac{z^{2}}{2}

(485)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}x+\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sgn(x)-\sigma z\right)\right)^{2}

(486)

\displaystyle\phi_{d}^{3}(x,z)

\displaystyle=|x|-\left\lvert\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)x+\sigma z\right\rvert+\frac{z^{2}}{2}

(487)

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}x-\sigma z+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sgn\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)x+\sigma z\right]\right)^{2}

(488)

\displaystyle\phi_{d}^{4}(x,z)

\displaystyle=|x|-\left\lvert x-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sgn(x)+\sigma z\right\rvert+\frac{z^{2}}{2}

(489)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sgn(x)-\sigma z+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sgn\left[x-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sgn(x)+\sigma z\right]\right)^{2}.

(490)

Let us denote

\displaystyle A_{1}:=\left\{x:0\leq x<\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\right\},\quad A_{2}:=\left\{x:-\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}<x\leq 0\right\},

(491)

\displaystyle A_{3}:=\left\{x:x\geq\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\right\},\quad A_{4}:=\left\{x:x\leq-\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\right\},

(492)

and

\displaystyle B_{1}:=\left\{z:0\leq\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)x+\sigma z<\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\right\},

(493)

\displaystyle B_{2}:=\left\{z:-\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}<\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)x+\sigma z\leq 0\right\},

(494)

\displaystyle B_{3}:=\left\{z:\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)x+\sigma z\geq\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\right\},

(495)

\displaystyle B_{4}:=\left\{z:\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)x+\sigma z\leq-\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\right\},

(496)

and

\displaystyle C_{1}:=\left\{(x,z):0\leq x-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sgn(x)+\sigma z<\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\right\},

(497)

\displaystyle C_{2}:=\left\{(x,z):-\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}<x-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sgn(x)+\sigma z\leq 0\right\}

(498)

\displaystyle C_{3}:=\left\{(x,z):x-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sgn(x)+\sigma z\geq\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\right\},

(499)

\displaystyle C_{4}:=\left\{(x,z):x-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sgn(x)+\sigma z\leq-\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\right\},

(500)

so that, up to a set of null measure,

\displaystyle R_{1}

\displaystyle=(A_{1}\cup A_{2})\cap(B_{1}\cup B_{2}),\qquad\qquad R_{2}=(A_{3}\cup A_{4})\cap(C_{1}\cup C_{2}),

(501)

\displaystyle R_{3}

\displaystyle=(A_{1}\cup A_{2})\cap(B_{3}\cup B_{4}),\qquad\qquad R_{4}=(A_{3}\cup A_{4})\cap(C_{3}\cup C_{4}).

(502)

Proposition 16
.

Take
X
a Laplace random variable and
Z
a standard normal random variable independent of
X
, then if
\sigma^{2}=\ell^{2}d^{-2/3}
, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}(X,Z)\right]=-\frac{\ell^{3}}{3\sqrt{2\uppi}}.

(503)

Proof.

Taking expectations of
\phi_{d}^{i}\mathbbm{1}_{R_{i}}
for
i=1,\dots,4
and exploiting the symmetry of the laws of
X
and
Z
, we can write

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{1}(X,Z)\mathbbm{1}_{R_{1}}(X,Z)\right]

(504)

\displaystyle\qquad=2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{1}}(X,Z)\right]

(505)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X+\sigma Z\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{2}}(X,Z)\right]

(506)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\left(\frac{2}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}-\frac{1}{\sigma^{4(m-1)}r^{2}}\right)X-\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)\sigma Z\right)^{2}\right)\right.

(507)

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\times\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{1}\cup B_{2}}(X,Z)\right],

(508)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{2}(X,Z)\mathbbm{1}_{R_{2}}(X,Z)\right]

(509)

\displaystyle\qquad=2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\sigma Z\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{1}}(X,Z)\right]

(510)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}+\sigma Z\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{2}}(X,Z)\right]

(511)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X+\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\sigma Z\right)\right)^{2}\right)\right.

(512)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.\times\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{1}\cup C_{2}}(X,Z)\right],

(513)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{3}(X,Z)\mathbbm{1}_{R_{3}}(X,Z)\right]

(514)

\displaystyle\qquad=2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{3}}(X,Z)\right]

(515)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X+\sigma Z\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{4}}(X,Z)\right]

(516)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{3}}(X,Z)\right]

(517)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{4}}(X,Z)\right],

(518)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{4}(X,Z)\mathbbm{1}_{R_{4}}(X,Z)\right]=2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}+\sigma Z\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{4}}(X,Z)\right].

(519)

Using the integrals in Appendix
D.4
and Lebesgue’s dominated convergence theorem, we find that for
\alpha=\beta=1/3
and
r\geq 0

\displaystyle\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{1}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=0

(520)

\displaystyle\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{2}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=-2\frac{\ell^{3}r}{4\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}e^{-z^{2}/2}z\mathrm{d}z=\frac{\ell^{3}r}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}

(521)

\displaystyle\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{3}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{3\ell^{3}r}{8\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}e^{-z^{2}/2}z\mathrm{d}z-\frac{\ell^{3}r}{8\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int^{+\infty}_{0}e^{-z^{2}/2}z\mathrm{d}z=-\frac{\ell^{3}r}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}

(522)

\displaystyle\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{4}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\ell^{3}}{6\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}e^{-z^{2}/2}z^{3}\mathrm{d}z=-\frac{\ell^{3}}{3\sqrt{2\uppi}},

(523)

which gives

\displaystyle\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{1}(X,Z)\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{2}(X,Z)\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{3}(X,Z)\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{4}(X,Z)\right]\right)

(524)

\displaystyle=-\frac{\ell^{3}}{3\sqrt{2\uppi}}.

(525)

For
\alpha=1/3,\beta=m/3
for
m>1
and
r\geq 0
we have

\displaystyle\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{1}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=0

(526)

\displaystyle\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{2}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=0

(527)

\displaystyle\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{3}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=0

(528)

\displaystyle\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{4}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\ell^{3}}{6\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}e^{-z^{2}/2}z^{3}\mathrm{d}z=-\frac{\ell^{3}}{3\sqrt{2\uppi}},

(529)

which gives

\displaystyle\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{1}(X,Z)\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{2}(X,Z)\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{3}(X,Z)\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{4}(X,Z)\right]\right)

(530)

\displaystyle=-\frac{\ell^{3}}{3\sqrt{2\uppi}}.

(531)

∎

Proposition 17
.

Take
X
a Laplace random variable and
Z
a standard normal random variable independent of
X
, then if
\sigma^{2}=\ell^{2}d^{-2/3}

\displaystyle\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\var\left(\phi_{d}(X,Z)\right)=\frac{2\ell^{3}}{3\sqrt{2\uppi}}.

(532)

Proof.

As a consequence of the previous Proposition we have

\displaystyle\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}(X,Z)\right]^{2}=0.

(533)

Then, because
R_{j}\cap R_{i}=\emptyset
for all
j\neq i
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}(X,Z)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{1}(X,Z)^{2}R_{1}(X,Z)\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{2}(X,Z)^{2}R_{2}(X,Z)\right]

(534)

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{3}(X,Z)^{2}R_{3}(X,Z)\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{4}(X,Z)^{2}R_{4}(X,Z)\right],

(535)

and, exploiting again the symmetry of the laws of
X
and
Z
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{1}(X,Z)^{2}R_{1}(X,Z)\right]

(536)

\displaystyle=2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z+\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\left(\frac{2}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}-\frac{1}{\sigma^{4(m-1)}r^{2}}\right)X-\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)\sigma Z\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\right.

(537)

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\times\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{1}}(X,Z)\right],

(538)

\displaystyle+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z+\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\left(\frac{2}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}-\frac{1}{\sigma^{4(m-1)}r^{2}}\right)X-\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)\sigma Z\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\right.

(539)

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\times\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{2}}(X,Z)\right],

(540)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{2}(X,Z)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{R_{2}}(X,Z)\right]

(541)

\displaystyle=2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\sigma Z+\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X+\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\sigma Z\right)\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{1}}(X,Z)\right]

(542)

\displaystyle+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}+\sigma Z+\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X+\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\sigma Z\right)\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\right.

(543)

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\times\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{2}}(X,Z)\right],

(544)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{3}(X,Z)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{R_{3}}(X,Z)\right]

(545)

\displaystyle=2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z+\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{3}}(X,Z)\right]

(546)

\displaystyle+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X+\sigma Z+\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{4}}(X,Z)\right],

(547)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{4}(X,Z)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{R_{4}}(X,Z)\right]=2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}+\sigma Z\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{4}}(X,Z)\right].

(548)

Proceeding as for Proposition
16
,
using the integrals in Appendix
D.4
and Lebesgue’s dominated convergence theorem we can then show that for
\alpha=1/3,\beta=m/3
for
m\geq 1
and
r\geq 0

\displaystyle\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\var\left(\phi_{d}(X,Z)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{2\ell^{3}}{3\sqrt{2\uppi}}.

(549)

∎

Proposition 18
.

Take
X
a Laplace random variable and
Z
a standard normal random variable independent of
X
, then if
\sigma^{2}=\ell^{2}d^{-2/3}
we have

\displaystyle\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}(X,Z)^{3}\right]=0.

(550)

Proof.

Following the same structure of the previous propositions we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(X,Z)^{3}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{1}(X,Z)^{3}R_{1}(X,Z)\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{3}(X,Z)^{2}R_{2}(X,Z)\right]

(551)

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{3}(X,Z)^{3}R_{3}(X,Z)\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{4}(X,Z)^{3}R_{4}(X,Z)\right],

(552)

exploiting again the symmetry of the laws of
X
and
Z
, using the integrals in Appendix
D.4
, the dominated convergence theorem we can then show that

\displaystyle\lim_{d\to+\infty}d\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}(X,Z)^{3}\right]

\displaystyle=0.

(553)

∎

D.2
Bound on second moment of acceptance ratio for the Laplace distribution

Lemma 3
.

Let
Z
be a standard normal random variable and
\sigma=\ell/d^{\alpha}
for

\alpha=1/3
. Then, there exists a constant
C>0
such that for all
a\in\real

and
d\in\mathbb{N}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}(a,Z)^{2}\right]\leq\frac{C}{d^{2\alpha}}.

(554)

Proof.

We consider the case
a\geq 0
and
r\geq\sigma^{2(v-1)}
only, all the other cases follow from identical arguments. As in the derivation of the moments of
\phi_{d}
in Appendix
D.1
, we distinguish four regions.
We recall that
\sigma=\ell/d^{\alpha}
for
\alpha=1/3
and thus
\sigma^{p+1}\leq\sigma^{p}
for all
p\in\mathbb{N}
.
Take
r\geq\sigma^{-2(v-1)}
, for
R_{1}
, we have, using Hölder’s inequality multiple times,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{1}(a,Z)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{R_{1}}(a,Z)\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{1}(a,Z)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{1}\cup B_{2}}(a,Z)\right]

(555)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C\sigma^{2}\int_{-\left(1-1/\sigma^{2(v-1)}r\right)a/\sigma}^{\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-\left(1-1/\sigma^{2(v-1)}r\right)a}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2v-1}r}a-z\right)^{2}\right.

(556)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.+\left(\frac{z^{2}}{2\sigma}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{3}}\left(\left(\frac{2}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}-\frac{1}{\sigma^{4(m-1)}r^{2}}\right)a-\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)\sigma z\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\right]\mathrm{d}z

(557)

\displaystyle\qquad+C\sigma^{2}\int^{-\left(1-1/\sigma^{2(v-1)}r\right)a/\sigma}_{-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-\left(1-1/\sigma^{2(v-1)}r\right)a}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left[\left(\frac{2a}{\sigma}-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2v-1}r}a+z\right)^{2}\right.

(558)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.+\left(\frac{z^{2}}{2\sigma}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{3}}\left(\left(\frac{2}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}-\frac{1}{\sigma^{4(m-1)}r^{2}}\right)a-\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)\sigma z\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\right]\mathrm{d}z

(559)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C\sigma^{2}\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left(4\left(\frac{a}{\sigma}\right)^{2}+2\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2v-1}r}a\right)^{2}+2z^{2}\right)\mathrm{d}z

(560)

\displaystyle\qquad+C\sigma^{2}\int_{-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-\left(1-1/\sigma^{2(v-1)}r\right)a}^{\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-\left(1-1/\sigma^{2(v-1)}r\right)a}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}

(561)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\left(\frac{z^{2}}{2\sigma}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{3}}\left(\left(\frac{2}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}-\frac{1}{\sigma^{4(m-1)}r^{2}}\right)a-\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)\sigma z\right)^{2}\right)^{2}

(562)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C\sigma^{2}+C\sigma^{2}\int_{-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-\left(1-1/\sigma^{2(v-1)}r\right)a}^{\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-\left(1-1/\sigma^{2(v-1)}r\right)a}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}

(563)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\left(\frac{z^{4}}{4\sigma^{2}}+\frac{1}{4\sigma^{6}}\left(\left(\frac{2}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}-\frac{1}{\sigma^{4(m-1)}r^{2}}\right)^{4}a^{4}+\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)^{4}\sigma^{4}z^{4}\right)\right)\mathrm{d}z

(564)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C\sigma^{2},

(565)

where we used the fact that the moments of
Z
are bounded and
a\leq\sigma^{2v}r/2
for the first term, and the fact that
z\leq\sigma^{2v-1}r/2
for the second one.
Proceeding as above, for
R_{3}
,
a>0
and
r\geq\sigma^{-2(v-1)}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{3}(a,Z)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{R_{3}}(a,Z)\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{3}(a,Z)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{3}\cup B_{4}}(a,Z)\right]

(566)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C\sigma^{2}\int_{\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-\left(1-1/\sigma^{2(v-1)}r\right)a/\sigma}^{+\infty}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2v-1}r}a-z\right)^{2}\right.

(567)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.+\left(\frac{z^{2}}{2\sigma}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{3}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}a-\sigma z+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\right]\mathrm{d}z

(568)

\displaystyle\qquad+C\sigma^{2}\int^{-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-\left(1-1/\sigma^{2(v-1)}r\right)a/\sigma}_{-\infty}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left[\left(\frac{2a}{\sigma}-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2v-1}r}a+z\right)^{2}\right.

(569)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.+\left(\frac{z^{2}}{2\sigma}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{3}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}a-\sigma z+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\right]\mathrm{d}z

(570)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C\sigma^{2}+C\sigma^{2}\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left(\frac{1}{2\sigma^{3}}\left(\frac{a^{2}}{\sigma^{4(m-1)}r^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{4}}{4}-\sigma^{3}z+\frac{a}{\sigma^{2v-4}r}-\frac{2az}{\sigma^{2v-3}r}\right)\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}z

(571)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C\sigma^{2},

(572)

where we used again the boundedness of the moments of
Z
, the fact that
a\leq\sigma^{2v}r/2
and that
\sigma^{p+1}\leq\sigma^{p}
.
For
R_{2}
and
a>0
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{2}(a,Z)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{R_{2}}(a,Z)\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{2}(a,Z)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{C_{1}\cup C_{2}}(a,Z)\right]

(573)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C\sigma^{2}\int_{\sigma/2-a/\sigma}^{\sigma/2+\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-a/\sigma}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left[\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\sigma z\right)^{2}\right.

(574)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.+\left(\frac{z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}a+\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\sigma z\right)\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\right]\mathrm{d}z

(575)

\displaystyle\qquad+C\sigma^{2}\int^{\sigma/2-a/\sigma}_{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-a/\sigma}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left[\left(2a-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}+\sigma z\right)^{2}\right.

(576)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.+\left(\frac{z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}a+\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\sigma z\right)\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\right]\mathrm{d}z.

(577)

The first integral is bounded using the moments of
Z
, while for the third one let us denote
\chi(a,\sigma,z):=a-\sigma^{2}/2+\sigma z
, then

\displaystyle\int^{\sigma/2+\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-a/\sigma}_{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-a/\sigma}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left(\frac{z^{2}}{2\sigma}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{3}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}a+\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\sigma z\right)\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}z

(578)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\int^{\sigma/2+\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-a/\sigma}_{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-a/\sigma}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left(\frac{z^{2}}{2\sigma}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{3}}\left(\frac{\chi(a,\sigma,z)}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\sigma z\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}z

(579)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\leq C\int^{\sigma/2+\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-a/\sigma}_{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-a/\sigma}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left(\frac{z^{2}}{2\sigma}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{3}}\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\sigma z\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}z

(580)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+C\int^{\sigma/2+\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-a/\sigma}_{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-a/\sigma}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left(\frac{\chi(a,\sigma,z)^{2}}{2\sigma^{4m-1}r^{2}}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{\chi(a,\sigma,z)}{r\sigma^{2v-1}}\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\sigma z\right)\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}z;

(581)

recalling that in
R_{2}
we have
|\chi(a,\sigma,z)|\leq\sigma^{2v}r/2
, we obtain that this term is also bounded by
C\sigma^{2}
.
For
R_{4}
and
a>0
