, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{4}(a,Z)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{R_{4}}(a,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{4}(a,Z)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{C_{4}}(a,Z)\right]

(582)

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-a/\sigma}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left(2a-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}+\sigma z\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}z

(583)

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2-a/\sigma}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left(\frac{2a}{\sigma}-\frac{\sigma}{2}+z\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}z

(584)

Collecting all the terms together, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}(a,Z)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{4}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{i}(a,Z)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{T_{i}}(a,Z)\right]

(585)

\displaystyle\leq C\sigma^{2}+C\sigma^{2}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-a/\sigma}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left(\frac{2a}{\sigma}-\frac{\sigma}{2}+z\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}z.

(586)

Recall that
\sigma=\ell d^{-1/3}
. To bound the last integral we use Hölder’s inequality

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-a/\sigma}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left(\frac{2a}{\sigma}-\frac{\sigma}{2}+z\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-a/\sigma}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left[\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}+z\right)^{2}+4\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}-\frac{a}{\sigma}\right)^{2}\right]\mathrm{d}z

(587)

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-a/\sigma}\frac{e^{-z^{2}/2}}{\sqrt{2\uppi}}\left[\left(\frac{\ell^{2}}{4}+z^{2}\right)+4\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}-\frac{a}{\sigma}\right)^{2}\right]\mathrm{d}z.

(588)

The first term is bounded since the moments of
Z
are bounded. For the second term we use an estimate of the Gaussian cumulative distribution function. Let
\kappa(\ell,d,a):=\ell d^{-1/3}/2-ad^{1/3}/\ell
.
When
z<\kappa(\ell,d,a)<0
, we have
1<z/\kappa(\ell,d,a)
and therefore

\displaystyle(2\uppi)^{-1/2}\kappa(\ell,d,a)^{2}\int_{-\infty}^{\kappa(\ell,d,a)}e^{-z^{2}/2}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\leq(2\uppi)^{-1/2}\kappa(\ell,d,a)\int_{-\infty}^{\kappa(\ell,d,a)}ze^{-z^{2}/2}\mathrm{d}z,

(589)

\displaystyle=(2\uppi)^{-1/2}\kappa(\ell,d,a)\exp(-\kappa(\ell,d,a)^{2}/2).

(590)

However
y\mapsto ye^{-y^{2}/2}
is bounded over , therefore
(a,d)\longmapsto(2\uppi)^{-1/2}\kappa(\ell,d,a)\exp(-\kappa(\ell,d,a)^{2}/2)
is bounded over
\real{}_{+}^{*}\times\mathbb{N}
. If
\kappa(\ell,d,a)\geq 0
, then we still have
\kappa(\ell,d,a)<\ell
and thus have the inequality

(2\uppi)^{-1/2}\kappa(\ell,d,a)^{2}\int_{-\infty}^{\kappa(\ell,d,a)}e^{-z^{2}/2}\mathrm{d}z\leq(2\uppi)^{-1/2}\ell^{2}\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}e^{-z^{2}/2}\mathrm{d}z=\ell^{2}.

(591)

The result then follows since
\sigma=\ell d^{-1/3}
.

∎

D.3
Additional integrals for the Laplace distribution

We collect here two auxiliary Lemmata which are used in the proof of Proposition
15
.

Lemma 4
.

Take
X
a Laplace random variable and
Z
a standard normal random variable independent of
X
. Let
\tilde{X}:=X-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X\mathbbm{1}\left\{|X|<\sigma^{2v}r/2\right\}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sgn(X)\mathbbm{1}\left\{|X|\geq\sigma^{2v}r/2\right\}+\sigma Z
, then, for
\sigma=\ell d^{-\alpha}
with
\alpha=1/3
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left\{\sgn(X)\neq\sgn(\tilde{X})\right\}\right]\to 0

(592)

if
d\to\infty
.

Proof.

Using the same strategy of Appendix
D.1
and the symmetry of the laws of
X,Z
, we find that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left\{\sgn(X)\neq\sgn(\tilde{X})\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=2\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{2}}(X,Z)\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{2}}(X,Z)\right]

(593)

\displaystyle+2\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{4}}(X,Z)\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{4}}(X,Z)\right].

(594)

Using the same strategy used to obtain the moments of
\phi_{d}
in Appendix
D.1
, we find that

\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{2}}(X,Z)\right]=o(1),

in addition

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{2}}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\int_{\sigma^{2}/2-\sigma z}^{\sigma^{2}/2-\sigma z+\sigma^{2v}r/2}e^{-x}\mathrm{d}x\ \mathrm{d}z+o(1)

(595)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[\frac{\sigma^{2}r}{2}\delta_{m1}+...\right]\mathrm{d}z+o(1),

(596)

where
\delta_{m1}
is a Dirac’s delta, and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{4}}(X,Z)\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{4}}(X,Z)\right]

(597)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r}e^{-z^{2}/2}\int_{0}^{\sigma^{2}/2-\sigma z-\sigma^{2v}r/2}e^{-x}\mathrm{d}x\ \mathrm{d}z+o(1)

(598)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[-\sigma z+...\right]\mathrm{d}z+o(1).

(599)

Since
\sigma=\ell d^{-1/3}
and the remainder terms of the Taylor expansions are bounded, Lebesque’s dominated convergence theorem gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{2}}(X,Z)\right]\to 0,

(600)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{4}}(X,Z)\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{4}}(X,Z)\right]\to 0

(601)

as
d\to\infty
.
∎

Lemma 5
.

Take
X
a Laplace random variable and
Z
a standard normal random variable independent of
X
. Then,

d^{\alpha}\mathbb{E}\left[|Z|\left\lvert\phi_{d}\left(X,Z\right)\right\rvert\right]\to 0

(602)

for
\alpha=1/3
.

Proof.

Using Cauchy-Schwarz’s inequality we have that

\mathbb{E}\left[|Z|\left\lvert\phi_{d}\left(X,Z\right)\right\rvert\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[Z^{2}\right]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}(X,Z)^{2}\right]^{1/2};

(603)

the first expectation is equal to one, and the second one converges to zero at rate
d^{1/2}
by Proposition
17
. The result follows straightforwardly.
∎

D.4
Integrals for moment computations

We distinguish the case
v=1/2
and
v\geq 1
since the integration bounds significantly differ in these two cases. For values between
1/2
and
1
the integrals are not finite.
The expectations below are obtained by integrating w.r.t.
x
and using a Taylor expansion about
\sigma=0
to obtain the leading order terms. Using the Lagrange form of the remainder for the Taylor expansions, we find that the remainder terms are all of the form
\sigma^{1/\alpha+1}f(\gamma(\sigma,z))/(1/\alpha+1)!
where
\gamma(\sigma,z)
is a point between the limits of integration w.r.t.
x
and
f:x\mapsto p(x)e^{-x}
, where
p
is a polynomial.
Therefore, using the boundedness of the remainder and Lebesgue’s dominated convergence theorem, the integrals w.r.t.
z
of the remainder terms all converge to 0.

D.4.1
First moment

For simplicity, we only consider the case for
r\geq\sigma^{-2(v-1)}
, the other case follows analogously.

Region
R_{1}

Let us consider
\phi_{d}^{1}
first. We have

\displaystyle A_{1}\cap B_{1}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}0\leq x\leq\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\qquad\textrm{if }0\leq z\leq\frac{\sigma}{2}\\
0\leq x\leq\left(\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}-\sigma z\right)\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)^{-1}\qquad\textrm{if }\frac{\sigma}{2}\leq z\leq\frac{\sigma^{2v-1}r}{2}\\
-\sigma z\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)^{-1}\leq x\leq\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\qquad\textrm{if }\frac{\sigma}{2}-\frac{\sigma^{2v-1}r}{2}\leq z\leq 0\end{cases},

(604)

\displaystyle A_{1}\cap B_{2}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}0\leq x\leq\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\qquad\textrm{if }-\frac{\sigma^{2v-1}r}{2}\leq z\leq\frac{\sigma}{2}-\frac{\sigma^{2v-1}r}{2}\\
0\leq x\leq-\sigma z\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)^{-1}\qquad\textrm{if }\frac{\sigma}{2}-\frac{\sigma^{2v-1}r}{2}\leq z\leq 0\\
-\left(\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}+\sigma z\right)\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)^{-1}\leq x\leq\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\qquad\textrm{if }\frac{\sigma}{2}-\sigma^{2v-1}r\leq z\leq-\frac{\sigma^{2v-1}r}{2}\end{cases},

(605)

and

\displaystyle A_{1}\cap(B_{1}\cup B_{2})

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}0\leq x\leq\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\qquad\textrm{if }-\frac{\sigma^{2v-1}r}{2}\leq z\leq\frac{\sigma}{2}\\
0\leq x\leq\left(\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}-\sigma z\right)\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)^{-1}\qquad\textrm{if }\frac{\sigma}{2}\leq z\leq\frac{\sigma^{2v-1}r}{2}\\
-\left(\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}+\sigma z\right)\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)^{-1}\leq x\leq\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\\
\qquad\qquad\textrm{if }\frac{\sigma}{2}-\sigma^{2v-1}r\leq z\leq-\frac{\sigma^{2v-1}r}{2}\end{cases}.

(606)

The corresponding expectations are

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{X}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}-\sigma Z\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{1}}(X,Z)\right]

(607)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma/2}^{\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[z^{4-2v}\sigma^{4-2v}\xi(r)+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(608)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma/2-r\sigma^{2v-1}/2}^{0}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[z^{4-2v}\sigma^{4-2v}\xi(r)+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(609)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+o(1),

(610)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{X}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}+\sigma Z\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{2}}(X,Z)\right]

(611)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}^{0}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[z^{4-2v}\sigma^{4-2v}\xi(r)+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(612)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r}^{-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[z^{4-2v}\sigma^{4-2v}\xi(r)+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(613)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+o(1),

(614)

where
\xi:[0,+\infty)\to\real
is a function of
r
only which might change from one line to the other,
and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\left(\frac{2}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}-\frac{1}{\sigma^{4(m-1)}r^{2}}\right)X-\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)\sigma Z\right)^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{1}\cup B_{2}}(X,Z)\right]

(615)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int^{\sigma/2}_{-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(616)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma/2}^{\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(617)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r}^{-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[z^{2}\sigma^{2}\xi(r)+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z,

(618)

where
\xi:[0,+\infty)\to\real
is a function of
r
only which might change from one line to the other.

Region
R_{2}

For
\phi_{d}^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle A_{3}\cap C_{1}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\sigma^{2}/2-\sigma z\leq x\leq-\sigma z+\sigma^{2}/2+\sigma^{2v}r/2\qquad\textrm{if }z<\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2\\
\sigma^{2v}r/2<x\leq-\sigma z+\sigma^{2}/2+\sigma^{2v}r/2\qquad\textrm{if }\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2\leq z\leq\sigma/2\end{cases},

(619)

\displaystyle A_{3}\cap C_{2}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\sigma^{2}/2-\sigma z-\sigma^{2v}r/2\leq x\leq\sigma^{2}/2-\sigma z\qquad\textrm{if }z<\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r\\
\sigma^{2v}r/2<x\leq\sigma^{2}/2-\sigma z\qquad\textrm{if }\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r<z<\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2\end{cases}

(620)

and

\displaystyle A_{3}\cap(C_{1}\cup C_{2})=\begin{cases}\sigma^{2v}r/2\leq x\leq\sigma^{2v}r/2+\sigma^{2}/2-\sigma z\qquad\textrm{if }\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r\leq z\leq\sigma/2\\
-\sigma^{2v}r/2+\sigma^{2}/2-\sigma z<x\leq\sigma^{2v}r/2+\sigma^{2}/2-\sigma z\\
\qquad\qquad\textrm{if }z<\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r\end{cases}.

(621)

The corresponding expectations are

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\sigma Z\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{1}}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[-\frac{rz}{2}\sigma^{2v+1}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(622)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}^{\sigma/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[z^{2}\sigma^{2}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z,

(623)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}+\sigma Z\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{2}}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[-\frac{rz}{2}\sigma^{2v+1}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(624)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[-\frac{rz}{2}\sigma^{2v+1}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z,

(625)

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X+\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\sigma Z\right)\right)^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{1}\cup C_{2}}(X,Z)\right]

(626)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r}_{-\infty}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[\frac{rz}{2}\sigma^{2v+1}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(627)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r}^{\sigma/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[-\frac{z^{3}}{2r}\sigma^{3-2v}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z.

(628)

Region
R_{3}

For
\phi_{d}^{3}
, we have, in the case
r\geq\sigma^{-2(v-1)}
,

\displaystyle A_{1}\cap B_{3}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}0\leq x\leq\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\qquad\textrm{if }z>\frac{\sigma^{2v-1}r}{2}\\
\left(\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}-\sigma z\right)\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)^{-1}<x\leq\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\qquad\textrm{if }\frac{\sigma}{2}\leq z\leq\frac{\sigma^{2v-1}r}{2}\end{cases},

(629)

\displaystyle A_{1}\cap B_{4}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}0\leq x\leq\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\qquad\textrm{if }z<\frac{\sigma}{2}-\sigma^{2v-1}r\\
0\leq x<\left(\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}-\sigma z\right)\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)^{-1}\qquad\textrm{if }\frac{\sigma}{2}-\sigma^{2v-1}r\leq z\leq-\frac{\sigma^{2v-1}r}{2}\end{cases}.

(630)

The corresponding expectations are

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z+\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{3}}(X,Z)\right]

(631)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}^{+\infty}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[-\frac{rz}{8}\sigma^{2v+1}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(632)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int^{\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}_{\sigma/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[z^{3-2v}\sigma^{3-2v}\xi(r)+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z,

(633)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X+\sigma Z+\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{4}}(X,Z)\right]

(634)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[\frac{3}{8}\sigma^{2v+1}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(635)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}^{-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[z^{3}\sigma^{3-2v}\xi(r)+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z,

(636)

where
\xi:[0,+\infty)\to\real
is a function of
r
only which might change from one line to the other.

Region
R_{4}

Finally, for
\phi_{d}^{4}
we have

\displaystyle A_{3}\cap C_{4}=\left\{z<\frac{\sigma}{2}-\sigma^{2v-1}r,\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}<x\leq\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\sigma z-\frac{\sigma^{2v}r}{2}\right\},

(637)

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}+\sigma Z\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{4}}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[\frac{z^{3}}{6}\sigma^{3}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z.

(638)

D.4.2
Second moment

For simplicity, we only consider the case for
r\geq\sigma^{-2(v-1)}
, the other case follows analogously.

Region
R_{1}

For
\phi_{d}^{1}
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{1}(X,Z)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{1}}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma/2}^{\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[z^{3}\sigma^{3}\xi(r)+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(639)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma/2-r\sigma^{2v-1}/2}^{0}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[z^{3}\sigma^{3}\xi(r)+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(640)

\displaystyle+o(1),

(641)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{1}(X,Z)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{2}}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}^{0}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[z^{3}\sigma^{3}\xi(r)+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(642)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r}^{-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[z^{3}\sigma^{3}\xi(r)+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(643)

\displaystyle+o(1),

(644)

where
\xi:[0,+\infty)\to\real
is a function of
r
only which might change from one line to the other.

Region
R_{2}

For
\phi_{d}^{2}
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{2}(X,Z)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{1}}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[\frac{rz^{2}}{2}\sigma^{2v+2}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(645)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}^{\sigma/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[-z^{3}\sigma^{3}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z,

(646)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{2}(X,Z)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{2}}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[z^{2}\sigma^{2v+2}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(647)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[-\frac{rz^{2}}{2}\sigma^{2v+2}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z.

(648)

Region
R_{3}

For
\phi_{d}^{3}
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z+\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{3}}(X,Z)\right]

(649)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}^{+\infty}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[\frac{rz^{2}}{24}\sigma^{2v+2}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(650)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int^{\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}_{\sigma/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[\frac{rz^{2}}{24}\sigma^{2v+2}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z,

(651)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X+\sigma Z+\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{4}}(X,Z)\right]

(652)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[\frac{7rz^{2}}{24}\sigma^{2v+2}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z

(653)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}^{-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[\frac{7rz^{2}}{24}\sigma^{2v+2}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z,

(654)

Region
R_{4}

For
\phi_{d}^{4}
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}+\sigma Z\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{4}}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[-\frac{z^{3}}{3}\sigma^{3}+\dots\right]\mathrm{d}z.

(655)

D.4.3
Third moment

Having established that the only possible scaling is given by
\alpha=1/3
,
\beta=m/3
with
m\geq 1
, we now proceed to bound the third moment of
\phi_{d}
in this case.
For simplicity, we only consider the case for
r\geq 1
, the other case follows analogously.

Since
m\geq 1
, we find that
\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{1}(X,Z)^{3}\mathbbm{1}_{R_{1}}(X,Z)\right]=o(1)
as
d\to\infty
since the limits of integration all converge to 0. Then, using Hölder’s inequality for
\phi_{d}^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{d}^{2}(X,Z)^{3}\right]\leq C\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\sigma Z\right)^{3}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{1}}(X,Z)\right]

(656)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+C\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}+\sigma Z\right)^{3}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{2}}(X,Z)\right]

(657)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+C\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X+\left(1-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}\right)\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\sigma Z\right)\right)^{2}\right)^{3}\right.

(658)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.\times\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{1}\cup C_{2}}(X,Z)\right]

(659)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\frac{C}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[-\frac{rz^{3}}{2}\sigma^{5}+...\right]\mathrm{d}z

(660)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{C}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[-\frac{rz^{3}}{2}\sigma^{5}+...\right]\mathrm{d}z

(661)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{C}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[z^{3}\sigma^{5}\xi(r)+...\right]\mathrm{d}z+o(1),

(662)

where
\xi:[0,+\infty)\to\real
is a function of
r
only which might change from one line to the other.
For
\phi_{d}^{3}
, we have, using again Hölder’s inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z+\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}\right)^{3}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{3}}(X,Z)\right]

(663)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\leq C\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z\right)^{3}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{3}}(X,Z)\right]

(664)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+C\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}\right)^{3}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{3}}(X,Z)\right]

(665)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}^{+\infty}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[-\frac{z^{3}r}{2}\sigma^{5}+...\right]\mathrm{d}z

(666)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}^{+\infty}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[z^{3}\sigma^{5}\xi(r)+...\right]\mathrm{d}z+o(1)

(667)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X+\sigma Z+\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}\right)^{3}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{4}}(X,Z)\right]

(668)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\leq C\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X+\sigma Z\right)^{3}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{4}}(X,Z)\right]

(669)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+C\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{Z^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2(v-1)}r}X-\sigma Z-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}\right)^{3}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{1}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{4}}(X,Z)\right]

(670)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}^{+\infty}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[\frac{z^{3}r}{2}\sigma^{5}+...\right]\mathrm{d}z

(671)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{\sigma^{2v-1}r/2}^{+\infty}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[z^{3}\sigma^{5}\xi(r)+...\right]\mathrm{d}z+o(1),

(672)

where
\xi:[0,+\infty)\to\real
is a function of
r
only which might change from one line to the other.
Finally, for
\phi_{d}^{4}
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2X-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}+\sigma Z\right)^{3}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{C_{4}}(X,Z)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\uppi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\sigma/2-\sigma^{2v-1}r}e^{-z^{2}/2}\left[\frac{z^{3}}{10}\sigma^{5}+...\right]\mathrm{d}z.

(673)

and
3
, respectively.
In the case
v=3,r=2

in
Figure

12
, we find that the behaviour for low values of
d
significantly differs from the limiting one.
For values of
d
lower than 130 the
\textrm{ESJD}_{d}
achieves its maximum at a value of the acceptance
a_{d}(\ell,r)
different from that predicted by
Theorem

3
. In practice, this might mean that for low dimensional
settings the recommended choice of

a(\ell,c)=0.360
is far from optimal. Similar behaviours for small
d
have also been observed in the case of RWM and MALA (e.g.,
[
44
, Section 2.1]
).

E.3
Mix of a Laplace and differentiable target

We collect here the rest of the numerical experiments illustrating the
scaling the results for the density defined in (
34
). Similarly to

Section

E.2
we consider three algorithmic settings, summarized in
Table
3
. All chains are initialized at stationarity drawing i.i.d samples from the target, i.e.,
X_{0}^{d}\sim\pi_{d}
, so that no burnin effects are present.

To sample from the distribution
\pi
in (
34
) we employ the following procedure.
Let
X
be a random variable, distributed according to the density
\pi(x)\propto e^{-\left|x\right|-x^{2}/2}
. Since this distribution is symmetrical,
X
has the same
distribution as
\varepsilon Z
, where
Z
has the same distribution as

\left|X\right|
and
\mathbb{P}(\varepsilon=1)=\mathbb{P}(\varepsilon=-1)=1/2
. The density of
Z
is given by

\pi_{Z}(z)\propto e^{-(z+1)^{2}/2}
for any positive
z
. However, if
Y
is a normal
distribution of mean

-1
and variance
1
,
Y
conditionally to the event
(Y>0)
admits, for all
y>0
,
y\mapsto(\sqrt{2\pi}\mathbb{P}(Y>0))^{-1}e^{-(y+1)^{2}/2}
as a
density.
Therefore, we sample
Z
by sampling the normal variable
Y
and rejecting whenever
Y<0
. In practice, when
Y<-2
, we don’t reject by taking
-Y-2
instead of
Y
.

Figure

Algorithm

\alpha

\beta

v

r

13

sG-MALA

1/3

1/3

1

0

14

P-MALA

1/3

1/3

1

1

4

MY-MALA

1/3

1

3

Table 3:
Algorithm setting for the simulation study on the mixed Laplace-normal target.

The first plot in Figures
14
–
13
shows
that for
\alpha\neq 1/3
the acceptance ratio
a_{d}(\ell,r)
becomes
degenerate as
d
increases; while the second plot shows that
(a_{d}(\ell,r))_{d\in\mathbb{N}^{*}}
and
(\textrm{ESJD}_{d})_{d\in\mathbb{N}^{*}}
converge as
d\to\infty
.

It is interesting to note that the convergence of these plots happens faster
with respect to the dimension
d
, compared to the Laplace distribution. It is also
surprising that out of the three settings tested for the parameters
r
and
v
, the
one that seems to converge faster, and that looks most stable, is
v=3
and
r=2
, which
was never the case for the other densities. This may suggest that for such distributions,
a general MY-MALA algorithm has better properties of convergence than sG-MALA and P-MALA.
